 Board of Governors of the Federal Reserve System
 The Federal Reserve, the central bank of the United States,
 provides the nation with a safe, flexible, and stable monetary and
 financial system.

 Effective June 24, 2019, the Federal Reserve Board staff will make a change
 to the indexation of the daily Broad, AFE, and EME dollar indexes. For more
 information, see the
 "Technical Q&As" .
